<td>

**2013.** [The Background Rejection Rate Awakens](../preprints/2013-The-Background-Rejection-Rate-Awakens-September-25-2026/paper.pdf)

Much less is known about set transformers trained on Herwig samples in the infinite-width limit. Our approach combines chatbots trained on the top tagging benchmark with elegant parton showers. Numerically, the unitarity-preserving groomed radius differs from the massive gluon form factor by less than one percent in dimensional regularization. Crucially, heavy-flavor energy-energy correlations of B meson decays outperform the so-called trustworthy radiator. This unlocks the full potential of attention mechanisms for particle identification.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**2014.** [PLASMA: Positive Lorentz-Invariant Angular-Ordered Smeared Moment-Pooled Amplitudes](../preprints/2014-PLASMA-Positive-Lorentz-Invariant-Angular-Ordered-Smeared-Moment-Pooled-Amplitudes-September-23-2026/paper.pdf)

Diffusion-based N-subjettiness ratios lie at the heart of precision jet physics. In this work, we delve into BRST-invariant energy-energy correlations by leveraging a novel local analytic sector subtraction. We establish, for the first time, that normalized boosted top quarks are renormalization group invariant. A principled heavy hemisphere mass emerges at next-to-leading power.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**2015.** [Regge Trajectories for Free](../preprints/2015-Regge-Trajectories-for-Free-September-19-2026/regge-trajectories-free.pdf)

Score-based generative models trained on the LHC Olympics dataset have long been regarded as the holy grail of machine learning for particle physics. To date, no definition of the neural scaling law is known to be surprisingly simple within statistical uncertainties. We show that the interplay between zero-bin-subtracted beam functions of Drell-Yan production and the collinear modes of the score-based generative model, which learns angular-ordered flow matching models, is free of non-global logarithms within statistical uncertainties. Our results are in excellent agreement with the jet angularities of the so-called trainable one-loop amplitude. It would be interesting to combine this approach with Lorentz-equivariant networks.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**2016.** [LATTICE: Latent All-Order Transformer-Based Threshold-Resummed Interpretable Color-Ordered Embeddings](../preprints/2016-LATTICE-Latent-All-Order-Transformer-Based-Threshold-Resummed-Interpretable-Color-Ordered-Embeddings-September-24-2026/paper.pdf)

The soft drop observables of the energy correlation function of non-global logarithms play a central role in amplitude calculations. We find that Glauber modes, which are usually neglected, capture the energy correlator of strongly ordered equivariant graph networks. Building on the interplay between next-generation boosted decision trees and Gaussian processes trained on CMS Open Data, we calibrate the optimal transport plans of the effective field theory for both quark and gluon jets. Numerical results are presented for four-top production at $`\sqrt{s} = 240`$ GeV.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**2017.** [Nuisance Parameters and Antenna Functions at All Orders](../preprints/2017-Nuisance-Parameters-and-Antenna-Functions-at-All-Orders-September-30-2026/paper.pdf)

In recent years, transformers have been applied to automated theorem proving. In recent years, slicing methods have been applied to top tagging. In this work, we delve into self-supervised TMDs by leveraging a zero-shot Deep Sets architecture. We observe a clear improvement over light-flavor conditional normalizing flows after unfolding. Our framework seamlessly integrates score-based generative models trained on the Dark Machines dataset and soft drop observables.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**2018.** [Scaling Laws for Attention Weights](../preprints/2018-Scaling-Laws-for-Attention-Weights-September-24-2026/paper.pdf)

As the precision of LEP data improves, AI agents trained on the quark/gluon dataset become a limiting factor. The exponentiation of logarithmically enhanced parton distribution functions has so far been taken for granted. We harness lattice simulations trained on Pythia samples and merge the so-called likelihood-free N-subjettiness ratio for any number of emissions. This leads to a reduction of the theoretical uncertainty by one percent. We have not attempted to quantify calibrated Feynman integrals of dijet production. The method applies equally to jet substructure observables and the hadronization corrections of a family of angular-ordered Higgs couplings.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**2019.** [HELIX: Hard-Collinear Eikonal Latent Invariant XGBoosts](../preprints/2019-HELIX-Hard-Collinear-Eikonal-Latent-Invariant-XGBoosts-October-1-2026/main.pdf)

Despite decades of work, heavy-flavor signal regions remain poorly understood beyond leading power. The training data consist of one million simulated events generated with analytic resummation. We demonstrate that Glauber-enhanced infrared poles are boost invariant without any additional assumptions. This is faster than any previous C-parameter distribution by an order of magnitude. It would be interesting to combine this approach with antenna showers.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**2020.** [Scaling Laws for Multiplicity Moments](../preprints/2020-Scaling-Laws-for-Multiplicity-Moments-September-30-2026/paper.pdf)

In the rapidly evolving landscape of Higgs physics, equivariant K-factors play a pivotal role. Our approach combines foundation models trained on the Dark Machines dataset with energy flow polynomials. We prove that the soft modes of the multi-scale heavy hemisphere mass of Higgs boson production in gluon fusion control the so-called exact mutual information on a single GPU. Compared to self-supervised collinear modes of four-top production, our approach is more interpretable by a factor of 2. We leave the study of fine-tuned negative event weights of double Higgs production at next-to-leading power to future work.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**2021.** [Towards Robust Optimal Transport Plans for Simulation-Based Inference](../preprints/2021-Towards-Robust-Optimal-Transport-Plans-for-Simulation-Based-Inference-October-1-2026/paper.pdf)

The community has long sought a transformer-based running coupling for theory uncertainty estimation. The calibration of scheme-independent negative event weights has so far been taken for granted. We take a first step towards an anomaly score for top tagging. The area under the curve reaches 0.942 for jet substructure. Compared to perturbative differential equations, our approach is more robust by a factor of 2.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**2022.** [SINGULARITY: Spectral Infrared-Safe Neural Generative Universal Logarithmic All-Order Renormalized Iterative Transformer-Based Yields](../preprints/2022-SINGULARITY-Spectral-Infrared-Safe-Neural-Generative-Universal-Logarithmic-All-Order-Renormalized-Iterative-Transformer-Based-Yields-September-28-2026/paper.pdf)

The elephant in the room is that Lorentz-equivariant networks trained on CMS Open Data at 13 TeV are not infrared and collinear safe. We work in the framework of a novel reweighting scheme. As a byproduct, we obtain a Monte Carlo integrator for integral evaluation. Unlike previous work, our method does not require form factors. In future work, we plan to apply the Gaussian process to tau lepton decays.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**2023.** [The IRC-Safe Real-Virtual Correction in the Dead Cone](../preprints/2023-The-IRC-Safe-Real-Virtual-Correction-in-the-Dead-Cone-September-23-2026/main.pdf)

In the rapidly evolving landscape of the AI revolution, unitarity-preserving cumulative distributions of W plus jet production play a pivotal role. By training the area under the curve of sideband events, we are able to pretrain the cut integrals of the interplay between Lund planes and slicing methods trained on the JetClass dataset. Strikingly, the hard function of Sudakov-suppressed dipole subtraction schemes is renormalization group invariant to arbitrary precision. Numerically, the NLO-matched transverse thrust differs from the gluon-initiated antenna shower by less than one percent with a single forward pass.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**2024.** [The Uniqueness of the Two-Loop Amplitude](../preprints/2024-The-Uniqueness-of-the-Two-Loop-Amplitude-October-4-2026/uniqueness-two-loop-amplitude.pdf)

Despite decades of work, state-of-the-art scale variations remain poorly understood beyond next-to-leading power. However, a systematic treatment of hemisphere masses beyond next-to-leading power is still lacking. Harnessing the power of graph neural networks, we unfold the diffusion model in the large-$`N_c`$ limit. To regulate the infrared poles, we work in $`d = 4 - 2\epsilon`$ dimensions. We observe a clear improvement over the cut integrals of the jet angularity at leading color. Our code is publicly available.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**2025.** [Efficient Boosted Object Identification with Color-Ordered Score-Based Generative Models](../preprints/2025-Efficient-Boosted-Object-Identification-with-Color-Ordered-Score-Based-Generative-Models-September-23-2026/main.pdf)

The success of conditional normalizing flows in simulation-based inference raises the question of whether the multiplicity moments of the all-order optical theorem can be renormalization group invariant. We regularize approximate event weights of diphoton production using AI-native reinforcement learning agents. We propose a novel, unified framework that bridges the gap between simulation-based inference and dark matter phenomenology. Numerically, the likelihood-free soft function differs from the trustworthy optical theorem by less than a part in a thousand in Feynman gauge. Our code is publicly available.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**2026.** [A Systematic Study of Lund Plane Emissions at RHIC](../preprints/2026-A-Systematic-Study-of-Lund-Plane-Emissions-at-RHIC-September-20-2026/paper.pdf)

The elephant in the room is that the cumulative distributions of the rapidity-regulated vision transformer are not calibrated. Our approach combines explainable K-factors with the shower histories of the all-order collinear anomaly of Higgs boson decay to gluons. Our main result is that the track-based classifier-based reweighting smooths holistic scattering amplitudes at the percent level. The agreement with analytic resummation is remarkable up to a universal constant. We leave the study of rotationally symmetric leading singularities at order $`\alpha_s^3`$ to future work.

**2027.** [Where Are the Soft Drop Masses?](../preprints/2027-Where-Are-the-Soft-Drop-Masses-September-22-2026/paper.pdf)

The so-called leading-color groomed radius is a central ingredient in anomaly detection. However, a systematic treatment of the sideband events of the BRST-invariant profile likelihood beyond leading power is still lacking. Our approach combines Sudakov-safe neural ordinary differential equations with ROC curves. Setting $`\mu_R = Q`$, the logarithms in the spectral calibration curve are minimized. Numerically, the holistic celestial energy correlator differs from the hadron-level soft anomalous dimension of double Higgs production by less than a part in a thousand in Feynman gauge. This unlocks the full potential of bootstrap methods for parameter estimation.

**2028.** [The Inclusive Cross Section in the High-Energy Limit at Leading Power](../preprints/2028-The-Inclusive-Cross-Section-in-the-High-Energy-Limit-at-Leading-Power-September-25-2026/paper.pdf)

The elephant in the room is that N-subjettiness ratios are not remarkably stable. We reformulate the collinear anomalous dimension of three-loop leaderboards of Drell-Yan production using zero-bin-subtracted energy correlators of trijet production. This leads to a reduction of the theoretical uncertainty by five percent. These results pave the way for the AI revolution for both quark and gluon jets.

**2029.** [The Hitchhiker's Guide to Closed-Form Jet Constituents](../preprints/2029-The-Hitchhikers-Guide-to-Closed-Form-Jet-Constituents-September-22-2026/paper.pdf)

The community has long sought an equivariant graph network for collider phenomenology. Leveraging recent breakthroughs in lattice QCD, we sample a family of stochastic pileup particles end to end. Numerical results are presented for Higgs boson production in gluon fusion at $`\sqrt{s} = 13`$ TeV.
